/**
 * Agentic Ads Lab batch mode (COD-833): run one procedure N times on each of a
 * set of lab repositories, grade every run with the production grader, and
 * report its perfect rate before the campaign gets traffic.
 *
 * An environment case prepares the chat's VM account before the accepted run's
 * agent starts. Each chat has its own Linux user and HOME, so a case changes
 * only that chat. Toolchains go under `~/.cache`, which Cloud checkpoints
 * exclude.
 */
```
- File `common/src/ads/agentic-bench-env.ts`:
```
/**
 * The bench's environment axis (bench-v2, B3): what the machine around the
 * repository looks like. Sampled, never crossed with every repository: the
 * planner (`agentic-bench-plan.ts`) picks each case's live cells by
 * `hash(skill.id, env)`, so successive versions of a skill hit the same cells.
 *
 * - `lab`: a Cloud Lab VM (Linux), set up by the Lab batch's environment case
 *   (`LAB_ENVIRONMENTS`).
 * - `desktop`: headless Freebuff Desktop on a GitHub runner
 *   (`sponsored-skill-bench.yml`), with real containment (bwrap and a private
 *   HOME on Linux), which the Lab cannot reproduce (the COD-825 shim failures).
 *   `setup` runs on the runner before the leg. It may append `KEY=VALUE` lines
 *   to `$BENCH_ENV_FILE` (the Desktop process's environment overrides; `PATH`
 *   replaces it) and set `BENCH_PROJECT_PARENT=<dir>` there, the folder the
 *   project is cloned under (paths with spaces, OneDrive, >260 characters).
 *
 * `hides` names the toolchains the case takes away. A cell whose run needs one
 * is labelled `should_fail_gracefully` by the oracle until managed toolchains
 * (spec E1) ship and `BENCH_HEALED_TOOLCHAINS` lists it.
 */
```
- File `common/src/ads/agentic-bench-families.ts`:
```
/** Each pool entry's language family, for "at least one pass per family". */
```
- File `common/src/ads/agentic-bench-oracle.ts`:
```
/**
 * The bench's static oracle (bench-v2, B2): what a skill SHOULD do on one
 * bench cell (repository snapshot × OS × environment), decided with no run:
 *
 * - `should_refuse`: the skill's own preflight refuses the repository (or
 *   the OS) before any edit. The real `runSponsoredSkillPreflight` decides
 *   it over a static context of the snapshot, and Track A's fit oracle
 *   (`sponsoredSkillFitRefusal` over `sponsoredRepoFacts`) must agree.
 * - `should_fail_gracefully`: the skill fits, but the environment takes away
 *   a toolchain the run needs (`BenchEnvironment.hides`), so the run should
 *   stop cleanly, not crash or report a delivery.
 * - `should_run`: neither; the run should deliver.
 *
 * Checks it cannot decide statically (commands, scripts, globs over source
 * files, lockfile text it does not keep) are `unverified`, never a refusal:
 * the live run decides them. Free and deterministic, so it covers the whole
 * pool on three OSes on every bench run.
 */
```
- File `common/src/ads/agentic-bench-plan.ts`:
```
/**
 * The bench planner (bench-v2, B4): which cells (pool entry × OS ×
 * environment) one bench run of a skill covers, and which of them run live.
 *
 * - The STATIC oracle always covers all of the pool on three OSes: free, and
 *   it catches a route or fit mistake on the extended set.
 * - LIVE runs use `core` entries only (sweeps add `extended`), and only cells
 *   the oracle labels `should_run`, plus a few `should_refuse` and
 *   environment cells to prove the refusal and the graceful stop.
 * - Every pick is by `hash(skill.id, …)`, never by version, so successive
 *   versions of a skill hit the same cells and their receipts compare.
 *
 * Modes (Owen, 2026-10-14):
 * - `smoke` (about 1h, ≤12 Lab runs): Lab `default` on 10 stratified
 *   `should_run` core repositories plus one run per smoke environment.
 * - `full` (about 3h of Lab plus a Desktop matrix): every Lab `should_run`
 *   core cell (failures re-run, up to 3 attempts), the full environments
 *   sampled, 3 live `should_refuse` cells, and Desktop shards (Linux about 12
 *   runs, Windows ≤18, macOS ≤6; macOS only on a minor or major bump).
 * - `sweep` (weekly, and when a skill is first seeded): `full` with the
 *   extended entries live too. It never gates a PR.
 */
```
- File `common/src/ads/agentic-bench-receipt.ts`:
```
/**
 * Bench receipts (bench-v2, B5): what one bench run of one skill version
 * found, committed next to the version as
 * `common/src/ads/sponsored-skills/<id>/<version>.bench.json` and indexed in
 * `sponsored-skills/receipts.ts`. CI checks the newest version of every skill
 * has a current one (`sponsored-skill-bench-receipt.test.ts`) and runs
 * nothing: the bench itself runs only when someone dispatches it.
 */
```

 * `bun scripts/agentic-ad-lab-bench-verify.ts --bench=v2 --snapshots` into
 * `agentic-bench-v2-snapshots.json`; a test checks every pool entry has one.
 */
```

- File `common/src/ads/agentic-ad-lab-batch.ts` (476 lines):
```
import { z } from 'zod/v4'
import { labBatchSelectionSchema } from './agentic-ad-lab'

/**
 * Agentic Ads Lab batch mode (COD-833): run one procedure N times on each of a
 * set of lab repositories, grade every run with the production grader, and
 * report its perfect rate before the campaign gets traffic.
 *
 * An environment case prepares the chat's VM account before the accepted run's
 * agent starts. Each chat has its own Linux user and HOME, so a case changes
 * only that chat. Toolchains go under `~/.cache`, which Cloud checkpoints
 * exclude.
 */

const LAB = '$HOME/.cache/freebuff-lab'
const RC_MARK = '# freebuff-lab toolchain'

/**
 * Shadow the VM's own Node binaries for this chat only. `~/.local/bin` is first
 * on every Cloud chat shell's PATH; a manager activated the usual way prepends
 * its own bin directory and so still wins.
 */
const HIDE_SYSTEM_NODE = `mkdir -p "$HOME/.local/bin"
for tool in node npm npx corepack; do
  printf '#!/bin/sh\\necho "sh: 1: %s: not found" >&2\\nexit 127\\n' "$tool" > "$HOME/.local/bin/$tool"
  chmod 755 "$HOME/.local/bin/$tool"
done`

/** Appends the activation a developer keeps in their shell profile, once. */
const profile = (
  lines: string,
) => `for rc in "$HOME/.bashrc" "$HOME/.profile"; do
  grep -qF '${RC_MARK}' "$rc" 2>/dev/null || printf '%s\\n' '${RC_MARK}' ${lines
    .split('\n')
    .map((line) => `'${line}'`)
    .join(' ')} >> "$rc"
done`

const NVM = `set -eu
export NVM_DIR="${LAB}/nvm"
mkdir -p "$NVM_DIR"
if [ ! -s "$NVM_DIR/nvm.sh" ]; then
  curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nvm-sh/nvm/v0.40.3/install.sh | PROFILE=/dev/null bash >/dev/null 2>&1
fi
set +u
. "$NVM_DIR/nvm.sh"
nvm install 22 >/dev/null 2>&1
nvm alias default 22 >/dev/null
set -u
${profile(`export NVM_DIR="$HOME/.cache/freebuff-lab/nvm"
[ -s "$NVM_DIR/nvm.sh" ] && . "$NVM_DIR/nvm.sh"`)}
${HIDE_SYSTEM_NODE}`

const MISE = `set -eu
export MISE_DATA_DIR="${LAB}/mise"
MISE="$MISE_DATA_DIR/bin/mise"
if [ ! -x "$MISE" ]; then
  curl -fsSL https://mise.run | MISE_INSTALL_PATH="$MISE" sh >/dev/null 2>&1
fi
"$MISE" use --global node@22 >/dev/null 2>&1
${profile(`export MISE_DATA_DIR="$HOME/.cache/freebuff-lab/mise"
eval "$("$MISE_DATA_DIR/bin/mise" activate bash)"`)}
${HIDE_SYSTEM_NODE}`

const VOLTA = `set -eu
export VOLTA_HOME="${LAB}/volta"
if [ ! -x "$VOLTA_HOME/bin/volta" ]; then
  curl -fsSL https://get.volta.sh | bash -s -- --skip-setup >/dev/null 2>&1
fi
"$VOLTA_HOME/bin/volta" install node@22 >/dev/null 2>&1
${profile(`export VOLTA_HOME="$HOME/.cache/freebuff-lab/volta"
export PATH="$VOLTA_HOME/bin:$PATH"`)}
${HIDE_SYSTEM_NODE}`

/** No Node anywhere a shell finds it: the case a managed toolchain (E1) heals. */
const NO_NODE = `set -eu
${HIDE_SYSTEM_NODE}`

const FNM = `set -eu
export FNM_DIR="${LAB}/fnm"
if [ ! -x "$FNM_DIR/fnm" ]; then
  curl -fsSL https://fnm.vercel.app/install | bash -s -- --install-dir "$FNM_DIR" --skip-shell >/dev/null 2>&1
fi
"$FNM_DIR/fnm" install 22 >/dev/null 2>&1
"$FNM_DIR/fnm" default 22 >/dev/null 2>&1
${profile(`export FNM_DIR="$HOME/.cache/freebuff-lab/fnm"
export PATH="$FNM_DIR:$PATH"
eval "$(fnm env --shell bash)"`)}
${HIDE_SYSTEM_NODE}`

/** asdf 0.16+ (one Go binary): Node reached only through its shims directory (COD-825's shape). */
const ASDF = `set -eu
export ASDF_DATA_DIR="${LAB}/asdf"
mkdir -p "$ASDF_DATA_DIR/bin"
if [ ! -x "$ASDF_DATA_DIR/bin/asdf" ]; then
  case "$(uname -m)" in aarch64|arm64) arch=arm64 ;; *) arch=amd64 ;; esac
  curl -fsSL "https://github.com/asdf-vm/asdf/releases/download/v0.18.0/asdf-v0.18.0-linux-$arch.tar.gz" | tar -xz -C "$ASDF_DATA_DIR/bin"
fi
export PATH="$ASDF_DATA_DIR/bin:$ASDF_DATA_DIR/shims:$PATH"
asdf plugin add nodejs >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
asdf install nodejs 22.12.0 >/dev/null 2>&1
(cd "$HOME" && asdf set --home nodejs 22.12.0)
${profile(`export ASDF_DATA_DIR="$HOME/.cache/freebuff-lab/asdf"
export PATH="$ASDF_DATA_DIR/bin:$ASDF_DATA_DIR/shims:$PATH"`)}
${HIDE_SYSTEM_NODE}`

/**
 * Debian/Ubuntu's PEP 668 marker, simulated for this chat: the system pip
 * refuses to install outside a virtual environment, with the message the
 * real one prints. `python3 -m pip` refuses too (pip's own
 * require-virtualenv). Inside a venv both work, as on a real machine.
 */
const PEP668 = `set -eu
mkdir -p "$HOME/.local/bin" "$HOME/.config/pip"
printf '[global]\\nrequire-virtualenv = true\\n' > "$HOME/.config/pip/pip.conf"
for tool in pip pip3; do
  cat > "$HOME/.local/bin/$tool" <<'SH'
#!/bin/sh
cat >&2 <<'MSG'
error: externally-managed-environment

x This environment is externally managed
\`-> To install Python packages system-wide, try apt install
 [freebuff product prompt text omitted]
    install.

    If you wish to install a non-Debian-packaged Python package,
    create a virtual environment using python3 -m venv path/to/venv.

note: If you believe this is a mistake, please contact your Python installation or OS distribution provider.
hint: See PEP 668 for the detailed specification.
MSG
exit 1
SH
  chmod 755 "$HOME/.local/bin/$tool"
done`

/** uv is the only Python: python, python3 and pip are not on PATH; uv and its managed 3.12 are. */
const UV_ONLY = `set -eu
export UV_INSTALL_DIR="${LAB}/uv"
if [ ! -x "$UV_INSTALL_DIR/uv" ]; then
  curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/0.9.5/install.sh | env UV_INSTALL_DIR="$UV_INSTALL_DIR" UV_NO_MODIFY_PATH=1 sh >/dev/null 2>&1
fi
UV_PYTHON_INSTALL_DIR="${LAB}/uv-python" UV_PYTHON_BIN_DIR="${LAB}/uv-python/bin" "$UV_INSTALL_DIR/uv" python install 3.12 >/dev/null 2>&1
${profile(`export PATH="$HOME/.cache/freebuff-lab/uv:$PATH"
export UV_PYTHON_INSTALL_DIR="$HOME/.cache/freebuff-lab/uv-python"`)}
mkdir -p "$HOME/.local/bin"
for tool in python python3 pip pip3; do
  printf '#!/bin/sh\\necho "sh: 1: %s: not found" >&2\\nexit 127\\n' "$tool" > "$HOME/.local/bin/$tool"
  chmod 755 "$HOME/.local/bin/$tool"
done`

/**
 * A second thread editing package.json while the sponsored run works in the
 * same checkout. Every 20 seconds, for at most 40 minutes, a detached writer
 * sets `freebuffLabConcurrentEdit` to its next count and logs that count
 * outside the checkout. The probe stops it and compares.
 */
const CONCURRENT_WRITER = `set -eu
mkdir -p "${LAB}"
rm -f "${LAB}/edits"
[ -f package.json ] || { echo 'No package.json in this repository; the collision case needs one.' >&2; exit 3; }
setsid nohup python3 -c '
import json, os, time
log = os.path.expanduser("~/.cache/freebuff-lab/edits")
for n in range(1, 121):
    time.sleep(20)
    try:
        with open("package.json") as f:
            data = json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        with open(log, "a") as f:
            f.write("unreadable\\n")
        continue
    data["freebuffLabConcurrentEdit"] = n
    with open("package.json", "w") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
        f.write("\\n")
    with open(log, "a") as f:
        f.write("%d\\n" % n)
' >/dev/null 2>&1 < /dev/null &
echo $! > "${LAB}/writer.pid"`

// setsid execs the writer in place, so the saved pid is the writer itself.
const CONCURRENT_PROBE = `if [ -s "${LAB}/writer.pid" ]; then
  kill "$(cat "${LAB}/writer.pid")" 2>/dev/null || true
  rm -f "${LAB}/writer.pid"
fi
python3 -c '
import json, os
log = os.path.expanduser("~/.cache/freebuff-lab/edits")
lines = open(log).read().split() if os.path.exists(log) else []
written = [int(x) for x in lines if x.isdigit()]
try:
    marker = json.load(open("package.json")).get("freebuffLabConcurrentEdit")
    valid = True
except Exception:
    marker, valid = None, False
last = written[-1] if written else None
print(json.dumps({
    "kind": "concurrent-package-json",
    "editsWritten": len(written),
    "unreadable": lines.count("unreadable"),
    "lastEdit": last,
    "markerInFile": marker if isinstance(marker, int) else None,
    "packageJsonValid": valid,
}))
'`

    probe: CONCURRENT_PROBE,
  },
} as const satisfies Record<
  string,
  {
    label: string
    description: string

export type LabEnvironment = keyof typeof LAB_ENVIRONMENTS
export const LAB_ENVIRONMENT_KEYS = Object.keys(LAB_ENVIRONMENTS) as [
  LabEnvironment,
  ...LabEnvironment[],
]

export const LAB_BATCH_MAX_REPETITIONS = 20
/** As many as a tester's lab holds, so one batch covers the whole bench. */
export const LAB_BATCH_MAX_REPOSITORIES = 30
/** Repositories a batch runs at once; each one's chats share its own VM. */
export const LAB_BATCH_PARALLEL_REPOSITORIES = 10
/** A batch's runs, all repositories together. */
export const LAB_BATCH_MAX_RUNS = 100

/**
 * How a batch answers a run's account step, which nobody is there to answer.
 * `mock` says Yes and connects {@link LAB_MOCK_CREDENTIAL} for every credential
 * the procedure declares, so the run continues past the step. Nothing checks
 * the placeholder against the advertiser: it proves the handoff and what the
 * agent does with a connected key, never that a real key works.
 */
export const LAB_BATCH_SIGNUPS = ['decline', 'confirm', 'mock'] as const
export type LabBatchSignup = (typeof LAB_BATCH_SIGNUPS)[number]
export const LAB_BATCH_SIGNUP_LABELS: Record<LabBatchSignup, string> = {
  decline: 'Answer No (account steps stay undone)',
  confirm: 'Answer Yes (browser sign-in only)',
  mock: 'Mock (Yes, with a placeholder key)',
}
/** Not a secret and not a real key: what a `mock` batch connects. */
export const LAB_MOCK_CREDENTIAL = 'freebuff-lab-mock-credential'

export const labBatchInputSchema = z
  .object({
    name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(120),
    /** The ad to test; its text is copied into the batch when it is created. */
    selection: labBatchSelectionSchema,
    projectIds: z
      .array(z.string().regex(/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,120}$/))
      .min(1)
      .max(LAB_BATCH_MAX_REPOSITORIES),
    repetitions: z.number().int().min(1).max(LAB_BATCH_MAX_REPETITIONS),
    environment: z.enum(LAB_ENVIRONMENT_KEYS),
    /** How the batch answers the run's signup question; no person is watching. */
    signup: z.enum(LAB_BATCH_SIGNUPS),
  })
  .strict()
  .refine(
    (input) =>
      new Set(input.projectIds).size === input.projectIds.length &&
      input.projectIds.length * input.repetitions <= LAB_BATCH_MAX_RUNS,
    `A batch runs at most ${LAB_BATCH_MAX_RUNS} times in all, on distinct repositories.`,
  )
export type LabBatchInput = z.infer<typeof labBatchInputSchema>

/** One graded run, as the production grader's tier ladder names it. */
export type LabRunGrade = {
  tier: 'perfect' | 'handoff' | 'partial' | 'broken' | 'lost'
  causeKey: string
  cause: string
  stepsCompleted: number | null
  stepsTotal: number | null
  grader: 'model' | 'rules'
  rubricVersion: string
}

export type LabConcurrencyProbe = {
  kind: 'concurrent-package-json'
  editsWritten: number
  unreadable: number
  lastEdit: number | null
  markerInFile: number | null
  packageJsonValid: boolean
}

export type LabBatchRun = {
  workspaceId: string
  projectId: string
  slot: number
  runId: string
  runStatus: string | null
  signup: string
  grade: LabRunGrade | null
  probe: LabConcurrencyProbe | { kind: 'setup-failed'; error: string } | null
}

export type LabBatch = {
  id: string
  name: string
  adName: string
  procedureSha256: string
  environment: LabEnvironment
  signup: LabBatchSignup
  repetitions: number
  /** `commit` is set when the repository was added pinned (`owner/name@sha`). */
  repositories: Array<{ projectId: string; fullName: string; commit?: string }>
  state: 'running' | 'stopped'
  createdAt: string
  runs: LabBatchRun[]
}

/**
 * A run whose writer edited package.json and whose last edit is missing
 * afterwards was overwritten from a stale copy, or the file was left invalid.
 * The writer's edit can land after the agent's last write, so the file
 * holding the LAST edit is the only clean outcome. Null: nothing to measure.
 */
export function labProbeCollided(probe: LabBatchRun['probe']): boolean | null {
  if (!probe || probe.kind !== 'concurrent-package-json') return null
  if (!probe.editsWritten) return null
  return (
    !probe.packageJsonValid ||
    probe.unreadable > 0 ||
    probe.markerInFile !== probe.lastEdit
  )
}

export type LabBatchSummary = {
  planned: number
  started: number
  graded: number
  perfect: number
  /** perfect / graded, or null before any run is graded. */
  perfectRate: number | null
  byTier: Record<LabRunGrade['tier'], number>
  byCause: Array<{ causeKey: string; count: number }>
  /** Graded runs by how far they got: `completed/total`, `unknown` when the grader could not count. */
  byStepReached: Array<{ reached: string; count: number }>
  collisions: { measured: number; collided: number } | null
}

export function summarizeLabBatch(batch: LabBatch): LabBatchSummary {
  const graded = batch.runs.flatMap((run) => (run.grade ? [run.grade] : []))
  const count = <K extends string>(keys: K[]) => {
    const totals = new Map<K, number>()
    for (const key of keys) totals.set(key, (totals.get(key) ?? 0) + 1)
    return [...totals].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1] || (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : 1))
  }
  const byTier = { perfect: 0, handoff: 0, partial: 0, broken: 0, lost: 0 }
  for (const grade of graded) byTier[grade.tier]++
  const probes = batch.runs.flatMap((run) => {
    const collided = labProbeCollided(run.probe)
    return collided === null ? [] : [collided]
  })
  return {
    planned: batch.repositories.length * batch.repetitions,
    started: batch.runs.length,
    graded: graded.length,
    perfect: byTier.perfect,
    perfectRate: graded.length ? byTier.perfect / graded.length : null,
    byTier,
    byCause: count(
      graded.filter((g) => g.tier !== 'perfect').map((g) => g.causeKey),
    ).map(([causeKey, n]) => ({ causeKey, count: n })),
    byStepReached: count(
      graded.map((g) =>
        g.stepsTotal === null || g.stepsCompleted === null
          ? 'unknown'
          : `${g.stepsCompleted}/${g.stepsTotal}`,
      ),
    ).map(([reached, n]) => ({ reached, count: n })),
    collisions:
      batch.environment === 'concurrent-package-json'
        ? {
            measured: probes.length,
            collided: probes.filter(Boolean).length,
          }
        : null,
  }
}

/** The first run-less slot: each repository's slots are claimed in order. */
export function nextLabBatchSlot(
  batch: Pick<LabBatch, 'repositories' | 'repetitions' | 'runs'>,
  busyProjects: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(),
): { projectId: string; slot: number } | null {
  for (let slot = 0; slot < batch.repetitions; slot++)
    for (const { projectId } of batch.repositories) {
      if (busyProjects.has(projectId)) continue
      if (
        !batch.runs.some(
          (run) => run.projectId === projectId && run.slot === slot,
        )
      )
        return { projectId, slot }
    }
  return null
}

```
import type { LabEnvironment } from './agentic-ad-lab-batch'

/**
 * The bench's environment axis (bench-v2, B3): what the machine around the
 * repository looks like. Sampled, never crossed with every repository: the
 * planner (`agentic-bench-plan.ts`) picks each case's live cells by
 * `hash(skill.id, env)`, so successive versions of a skill hit the same cells.
 *
 * - `lab`: a Cloud Lab VM (Linux), set up by the Lab batch's environment case
 *   (`LAB_ENVIRONMENTS`).
 * - `desktop`: headless Freebuff Desktop on a GitHub runner
 *   (`sponsored-skill-bench.yml`), with real containment (bwrap and a private
 *   HOME on Linux), which the Lab cannot reproduce (the COD-825 shim failures).
 *   `setup` runs on the runner before the leg. It may append `KEY=VALUE` lines
 *   to `$BENCH_ENV_FILE` (the Desktop process's environment overrides; `PATH`
 *   replaces it) and set `BENCH_PROJECT_PARENT=<dir>` there, the folder the
 *   project is cloned under (paths with spaces, OneDrive, >260 characters).
 *
 * `hides` names the toolchains the case takes away. A cell whose run needs one
 * is labelled `should_fail_gracefully` by the oracle until managed toolchains
 * (spec E1) ship and `BENCH_HEALED_TOOLCHAINS` lists it.
 */
export type BenchSurface = 'lab' | 'desktop'
export type BenchOs = 'linux' | 'win32' | 'darwin'
export type BenchToolchain = 'node' | 'python' | 'pip'

export type BenchEnvironment = {
  key: string
  surface: BenchSurface
  os: BenchOs
  /** smoke: in every smoke run; full: only in full runs and sweeps. */
  tier: 'smoke' | 'full'
  label: string
  /** The Lab batch's environment case (surface `lab`). */
  lab?: LabEnvironment
  /** Runs on the GitHub runner before a Desktop leg (surface `desktop`). */
  setup?: { shell: 'bash' | 'pwsh'; script: string }
  hides: BenchToolchain[]
  /** The case needs a root package.json (the collision writer edits it). */
  needsRootPackageJson?: boolean
}

/** Toolchains a run installs for itself once spec E1 ships; empty until then. */
export const BENCH_HEALED_TOOLCHAINS: readonly BenchToolchain[] = []

const lab = (
  key: LabEnvironment,
  tier: BenchEnvironment['tier'],
  label: string,
  hides: BenchToolchain[] = [],
  extra: Partial<BenchEnvironment> = {},
): BenchEnvironment => ({
  key: `lab:${key}`,
  surface: 'lab',
  os: 'linux',
  tier,
  label,
  lab: key,
  hides,
  ...extra,
})

const desktop = (
  os: BenchOs,
  key: string,
  label: string,
  setup: BenchEnvironment['setup'] | undefined,
  hides: BenchToolchain[] = [],
): BenchEnvironment => ({
  key: `desktop:${key}`,
  surface: 'desktop',
  os,
  tier: 'full',
  label,
  ...(setup ? { setup } : {}),
  hides,
})

/** PATH without any directory a Node install lives in (Linux and macOS runners). */
const POSIX_PATH_WITHOUT_NODE = `p=$(printf %s "$PATH" | tr ':' '\\n' | grep -v -e hostedtoolcache -e '/usr/local/bin' -e nvm -e volta -e '/opt/homebrew' -e fnm -e asdf -e mise | paste -sd: -)
echo "PATH=$p:/usr/bin:/bin" >> "$BENCH_ENV_FILE"`

/** A manager activated only from the profile, the way a developer's shell does it. */
const POSIX_MANAGER = (install: string, profile: string) => `set -eu
${install}
printf '%s\\n' ${profile
  .split('\n')
  .map((line) => `'${line}'`)
  .join(' ')} >> "$HOME/.bashrc"
printf '%s\\n' ${profile
  .split('\n')
  .map((line) => `'${line}'`)
  .join(' ')} >> "$HOME/.zprofile"
${POSIX_PATH_WITHOUT_NODE}`

const WIN_PATH_WITHOUT = (pattern: string) =>
  `$p = ($env:PATH -split ';' | Where-Object { $_ -and $_ -notmatch '${pattern}' }) -join ';'`

export function benchEnvironment(key: string): BenchEnvironment {
  const env = BENCH_ENVIRONMENTS.find((e) => e.key === key)
  if (!env) throw new Error(`No bench environment ${key}.`)
  return env
}

/** The case a surface and OS run when nothing else is asked for. */
export function benchDefaultEnvironment(
  surface: BenchSurface,
  os: BenchOs,
): BenchEnvironment {
  const key =
    surface === 'lab'
      ? 'lab:default'
      : os === 'win32'
        ? 'desktop:win-default'
        : os === 'darwin'
          ? 'desktop:mac-homebrew'
          : 'desktop:linux-default'
  return benchEnvironment(key)
}

import type { LabBenchV2Entry } from './agentic-ad-lab-bench-v2'

/** Each pool entry's language family, for "at least one pass per family". */
export function benchLanguageFamilies(
  pool: readonly LabBenchV2Entry[],
): ReadonlyMap<string, string> {
  return new Map(
    pool.map((entry) => {
      const { language } = entry.facets
      const family =
        language === 'python' || language === 'polyglot'
          ? 'python'
          : language === 'typescript' || language === 'javascript'
            ? 'node'
            : 'other'
      return [entry.key, family]
    }),
  )
}

- File `common/src/ads/agentic-bench-oracle.ts` (279 lines):
```
/**
 * The bench's static oracle (bench-v2, B2): what a skill SHOULD do on one
 * bench cell (repository snapshot × OS × environment), decided with no run:
 *
 * - `should_refuse`: the skill's own preflight refuses the repository (or
 *   the OS) before any edit. The real `runSponsoredSkillPreflight` decides
 *   it over a static context of the snapshot, and Track A's fit oracle
 *   (`sponsoredSkillFitRefusal` over `sponsoredRepoFacts`) must agree.
 * - `should_fail_gracefully`: the skill fits, but the environment takes away
 *   a toolchain the run needs (`BenchEnvironment.hides`), so the run should
 *   stop cleanly, not crash or report a delivery.
 * - `should_run`: neither; the run should deliver.
 *
 * Checks it cannot decide statically (commands, scripts, globs over source
 * files, lockfile text it does not keep) are `unverified`, never a refusal:
 * the live run decides them. Free and deterministic, so it covers the whole
 * pool on three OSes on every bench run.
 */
import {
  BENCH_HEALED_TOOLCHAINS,
  type BenchEnvironment,
  type BenchOs,
  type BenchToolchain,
} from './agentic-bench-env'
import {
  benchSnapshotForOs,
  type BenchSnapshot,
} from './agentic-bench-snapshot'
import {
  evaluateSponsoredSkillFit,
  sponsoredFitCheckDecidable,
  sponsoredRepoFacts,
  sponsoredStaticCheckContext,
  type SponsoredRepoFacts,
  type SponsoredSkillFitVerdict,
} from './sponsored-skill-fit'
import {
  sponsoredSkillFitEcosystems,
  sponsoredSkillGlob,
  sponsoredSkillPreflightChecks,
  type SponsoredSkill,
  type SponsoredSkillCheck,
} from './sponsored-skill'
import {
  runSponsoredSkillPreflight,
  SPONSORED_PREFLIGHT_OS_CHECK_ID,
} from './sponsored-step-driver'

export type BenchExpectation =
  | 'should_run'
  | 'should_refuse'
  | 'should_fail_gracefully'

export type BenchOracleVerdict = {
  expected: BenchExpectation
  /**
   * False when the environment cannot be set up on this repository (the
   * collision case needs a root package.json): the planner never runs it.
   */
  applicable: boolean
  /** One line: the refusing check, the hidden toolchain, or `fits`. */
  reason: string
  /** The fit oracle's verdict on the same facts. */
  fit: SponsoredSkillFitVerdict['fit']
  /** Preflight checks the snapshot cannot decide; the live run decides them. */
  unverified: string[]
  /**
   * The fit oracle and the real preflight disagree on a check both can
   * decide: a bug in one of them. The label follows the real preflight.
   */
  fidelity?: string
}

/** The toolchains a run of `skill` on this repository needs. */
export function benchRunToolchains(
  skill: SponsoredSkill,
  facts: SponsoredRepoFacts,
): BenchToolchain[] {
  const hasNode = facts.manifests.some((m) => m.ecosystem === 'npm')
  const hasPython = facts.manifests.some((m) => m.ecosystem === 'pypi')
  const routes = sponsoredSkillFitEcosystems(skill) ?? [
    ...new Set(
      skill.requirements.runtimes.map((r) =>
        r.name === 'python' ? 'python' : 'node',
      ),
    ),
  ]
  // The route this repository takes: the first the skill offers that the
  // repository has a manifest for, else the skill's first.
  const route =
    routes.find((r) => (r === 'node' ? hasNode : hasPython)) ?? routes[0]
  const needs = new Set<BenchToolchain>()
  if (route === 'node') needs.add('node')
  if (route === 'python') {
    needs.add('python')
    if (skill.packages.some((p) => p.ecosystem === 'pypi')) needs.add('pip')
  }
  // An npm tool needs Node only where a check runs it; instructions that use
  // it "when npx is available" do not make Node a requirement.
  const npmTools = new Set(
    skill.tools.filter((t) => t.source === 'npm').map((t) => t.name),
  )
  const checks = [
    ...sponsoredSkillPreflightChecks(skill),
    ...skill.steps.flatMap((step) => step.checks),
  ]
  if (
    checks.some(
      (check) =>
        check.kind === 'command_succeeds' &&
        (npmTools.has(check.argv[0] ?? '') ||
          ['node', 'npm', 'npx'].includes(check.argv[0] ?? '')),
    )
  )
    needs.add('node')
  return [...needs]
}

const contentless = (snapshot: BenchSnapshot) =>
  Object.entries(snapshot.files)
    .filter(([, text]) => text === null)
    .map(([path]) => path)

/** Whether the snapshot can decide this check at all. */
function staticallyDecidable(
  check: SponsoredSkillCheck,
  snapshot: BenchSnapshot,
): boolean {
  if (!sponsoredFitCheckDecidable(check)) return false
  if (check.kind === 'file_contains') {
    const re = sponsoredSkillGlob(check.path)
    if (contentless(snapshot).some((path) => re.test(path))) return false
  }
  return true
}

export async function benchRepoFacts(
  snapshot: BenchSnapshot,
): Promise<SponsoredRepoFacts> {
  return sponsoredRepoFacts({
    paths: Object.keys(snapshot.files),
    complete: snapshot.complete,
    read: (path) => snapshot.files[path] ?? null,
  })
}

/** THE label of one bench cell. */
export async function benchExpectation(
  skill: SponsoredSkill,
  snapshot: BenchSnapshot,
  os: BenchOs,
  env: Pick<BenchEnvironment, 'hides' | 'needsRootPackageJson'>,
): Promise<BenchOracleVerdict> {
  const onOs = benchSnapshotForOs(snapshot, os)
  const facts = await benchRepoFacts(onOs)
  const fit = evaluateSponsoredSkillFit(skill, { os, repo: facts })
  const applicable = !env.needsRootPackageJson || 'package.json' in onOs.files

  const files = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(onOs.files).map(([path, text]) => [path, text ?? '']),
  )
  const preflight = await runSponsoredSkillPreflight({
    skill,
    ctx: sponsoredStaticCheckContext(files),
    platform: os,
  })
  const declared = new Map(
    sponsoredSkillPreflightChecks(skill).map((check) => [check.id, check]),
  )
  const unverified: string[] = []
  const refused: string[] = []
  for (const result of preflight.checks) {
    if (result.status !== 'fail') continue
    if (result.id === SPONSORED_PREFLIGHT_OS_CHECK_ID) {
      refused.push(result.id)
      continue
    }
    const check = declared.get(result.id)
    if (check && staticallyDecidable(check, onOs)) refused.push(result.id)
    else unverified.push(result.id)
  }
  // A check the static run did not fail can still be one it could not
  // decide (a command it never ran because an earlier check failed).
  for (const check of declared.values())
    if (
      !staticallyDecidable(check, onOs) &&
      !unverified.includes(check.id) &&
      !refused.includes(check.id) &&
      check.kind !== 'no_privileged_secrets' &&
      check.kind !== 'changes_within'
    )
      unverified.push(check.id)

  let fidelity: string | undefined
  if (fit.fit === 'misfit' && !refused.length)
    fidelity = `the fit oracle refuses on ${fit.checkId} (${fit.reason}) but the real preflight passes every check it can decide`
  if (fit.fit === 'fits' && refused.length)
    fidelity = `the fit oracle says fits but the real preflight refuses on ${refused.join(', ')}`

  const base = {
    applicable,
    fit: fit.fit,
    unverified,
    ...(fidelity ? { fidelity } : {}),
  }
  if (refused.length)
    return {
      ...base,
      expected: 'should_refuse',
      reason: `preflight refuses: ${refused.join(', ')}`,
    }
  const missing = benchRunToolchains(skill, facts).filter(
    (tool) =>
      env.hides.includes(tool) && !BENCH_HEALED_TOOLCHAINS.includes(tool),
  )
  if (missing.length)
    return {
      ...base,
      expected: 'should_fail_gracefully',
      reason: `the environment has no ${missing.join(' or ')}`,
    }
  return { ...base, expected: 'should_run', reason: 'fits' }
}

/** What one live run of a cell did, from a Lab grade or a Desktop leg's result. */
export type BenchRunOutcome = {
  tier: 'perfect' | 'handoff' | 'partial' | 'broken' | 'lost' | null
  causeKey: string | null
  /** The step driver's stop reason when known (`preflight_failed`, …). */
  stoppedBecause?: string | null
  /** The run never tested the skill: no grade, a failed setup, an infra cause. */
  infra?: boolean
}

/** Grader causes that mean the run stopped at the skill's own refusal. */
export const BENCH_REFUSAL_CAUSES: readonly string[] = [
  'preflight_refused',
  'not_a_fit',
  'platform_unsupported',
]

/** Grader causes that mean a clean stop for a missing toolchain. */
export const BENCH_GRACEFUL_CAUSES: readonly string[] = [
  ...BENCH_REFUSAL_CAUSES,
  'missing_node',
  'missing_toolchain',
  'environment_limited',
]

export type BenchCellResult = 'pass' | 'fail' | 'mismatch' | 'infra'

/**
 * THE pass predicate of one live run against its label. A run that did
 * the opposite of its label (delivered where it should refuse, refused
 * where it should run) is a `mismatch`, always red.
 */
export function benchCellPass(
  expected: BenchExpectation,
  outcome: BenchRunOutcome,
): BenchCellResult {
  if (outcome.infra || !outcome.tier) return 'infra'
  const refused =
    outcome.stoppedBecause === 'preflight_failed' ||
    BENCH_REFUSAL_CAUSES.includes(outcome.causeKey ?? '')
  switch (expected) {
    case 'should_run':
      if (outcome.tier === 'perfect') return 'pass'
      return refused ? 'mismatch' : 'fail'
    case 'should_refuse':
      if (refused) return 'pass'
      return outcome.tier === 'perfect' ? 'mismatch' : 'fail'
    case 'should_fail_gracefully':
      if (outcome.tier === 'perfect') return 'mismatch'
      return refused || BENCH_GRACEFUL_CAUSES.includes(outcome.causeKey ?? '')
        ? 'pass'
        : 'fail'
  }
}
```
- File `common/src/ads/agentic-bench-plan.ts` (431 lines):
```
/**
 * The bench planner (bench-v2, B4): which cells (pool entry × OS ×
 * environment) one bench run of a skill covers, and which of them run live.
 *
 * - The STATIC oracle always covers all of the pool on three OSes: free, and
 *   it catches a route or fit mistake on the extended set.
 * - LIVE runs use `core` entries only (sweeps add `extended`), and only cells
 *   the oracle labels `should_run`, plus a few `should_refuse` and
 *   environment cells to prove the refusal and the graceful stop.
 * - Every pick is by `hash(skill.id, …)`, never by version, so successive
 *   versions of a skill hit the same cells and their receipts compare.
 *
 * Modes (Owen, 2026-10-14):
 * - `smoke` (about 1h, ≤12 Lab runs): Lab `default` on 10 stratified
 *   `should_run` core repositories plus one run per smoke environment.
 * - `full` (about 3h of Lab plus a Desktop matrix): every Lab `should_run`
 *   core cell (failures re-run, up to 3 attempts), the full environments
 *   sampled, 3 live `should_refuse` cells, and Desktop shards (Linux about 12
 *   runs, Windows ≤18, macOS ≤6; macOS only on a minor or major bump).
 * - `sweep` (weekly, and when a skill is first seeded): `full` with the
 *   extended entries live too. It never gates a PR.
 */
import {
  LAB_BATCH_MAX_REPOSITORIES,
  LAB_BATCH_MAX_RUNS,
  type LabEnvironment,
} from './agentic-ad-lab-batch'
import {
  LAB_BENCH_V2,
  LAB_BENCH_V2_VERSION,
  labBenchPinSpec,
  type LabBenchV2Entry,
} from './agentic-ad-lab-bench-v2'
import {
  BENCH_ENVIRONMENTS,
  benchDefaultEnvironment,
  type BenchEnvironment,
  type BenchOs,
  type BenchSurface,
} from './agentic-bench-env'
import {
  benchExpectation,
  type BenchExpectation,
  type BenchOracleVerdict,
} from './agentic-bench-oracle'
import { benchV2Snapshot } from './agentic-bench-v2-snapshot-data'
import { sponsoredSkillKey, sponsoredSkillSha256 } from './sponsored-skill'
import { sha256Hex } from '../util/hash'

import type { SkillBump } from './sponsored-skill-versions'
import type { SponsoredSkill } from './sponsored-skill'

export type BenchMode = 'smoke' | 'full' | 'sweep'
/** `first`: a skill with no earlier version (seeded): every surface, as a major. */
export type BenchBump = SkillBump | 'first'

export const BENCH_OSES: readonly BenchOs[] = ['linux', 'win32', 'darwin']

/** Planner constants: tunables, not env vars. */
export const BENCH_PLAN_LIMITS = {
  smokeLabRepositories: 10,
  /** Live attempts of a full-mode Lab `should_run` cell; a pass stops early. */
  fullAttempts: 3,
  fullEnvironmentCells: 2,
  fullRefusalCells: 3,
  desktop: {
    linux: { default: 8, perEnvironment: 1 },
    win32: { default: 12, perEnvironment: 1 },
    darwin: { default: 2, perEnvironment: 1 },
  },
  /** Lab chats at once: 3 batches × 3 chats each (`serverLabBatchRunner`). */
  /** Desktop runs per shard (one GitHub job): 4 × 30 min stays well inside a job's limit. */
  desktopShardEntries: 4,
  labConcurrentRuns: 9,
  labRunMinutes: 15,
  desktopRunMinutes: 30,
  /** GitHub bills macOS at 10×, Windows at 2× Linux minutes. */
  githubMinuteMultiplier: { linux: 1, win32: 2, darwin: 10 },
} as const

export type BenchCell = {
  /** `<entry>|<os>|<env>`: the same cell across versions and receipts. */
  id: string
  entry: string
  os: BenchOs
  surface: BenchSurface | 'static'
  env: string
  expected: BenchExpectation
  reason: string
  unverified: string[]
  fidelity?: string
  /** Live attempts planned; 0 for a static-only cell. */
  attempts: number
}

export type BenchLabBatchPlan = {
  environment: LabEnvironment
  /** `owner/name@<commit>`, as the skill-bench route takes them. */
  repositories: string[]
  cells: string[]
}

export type BenchDesktopShardPlan = {
  os: BenchOs
  env: string
  /** Pool keys: `owner/name` (cloned at its pin) or `fixture/<name>`. */
  entries: string[]
  cells: string[]
}

export type BenchPlan = {
  benchVersion: string
  mode: BenchMode
  bump: BenchBump
  skill: string
  skillSha256: string
  /** Every pool entry × OS, default environment, labelled by the oracle. */
  static: BenchCell[]
  live: BenchCell[]
  labBatches: BenchLabBatchPlan[]
  desktopShards: BenchDesktopShardPlan[]
  estimate: {
    labRuns: number
    desktopRuns: Record<BenchOs, number>
    labWallMinutes: number
    desktopWallMinutes: number
    githubMinutesLinuxEquivalent: number
  }
}

const cellId = (entry: string, os: BenchOs, env: string) =>
  `${entry}|${os}|${env}`

/** Deterministic order for picks: by hash of the skill id (not version) and the key. */
const byHash = <T>(
  skillId: string,
  salt: string,
  items: T[],
  key: (t: T) => string,
) =>
  [...items].sort((a, b) => {
    const ha = sha256Hex(`${skillId}|${salt}|${key(a)}`)
    const hb = sha256Hex(`${skillId}|${salt}|${key(b)}`)
    return ha < hb ? -1 : ha > hb ? 1 : 0
  })

/** The surfaces a mode and bump require (B5: patch → Lab + Windows; minor/major → all). */
export function benchSurfaces(
  mode: BenchMode,
  bump: BenchBump,
): Array<'lab' | `desktop-${BenchOs}`> {
  if (mode === 'smoke') return ['lab']
  return bump === 'patch'
    ? ['lab', 'desktop-linux', 'desktop-win32']
    : ['lab', 'desktop-linux', 'desktop-win32', 'desktop-darwin']
}

function toCell(
  entry: LabBenchV2Entry,
  os: BenchOs,
  surface: BenchCell['surface'],
  env: string,
  verdict: BenchOracleVerdict,
  attempts: number,
): BenchCell {
  return {
    id: cellId(entry.key, os, env),
    entry: entry.key,
    os,
    surface,
    env,
    expected: verdict.expected,
    reason: verdict.reason,
    unverified: verdict.unverified,
    ...(verdict.fidelity ? { fidelity: verdict.fidelity } : {}),
    attempts,
  }
}

/** Round-robin over buckets so a pick of n spans stacks, not one bucket. */
function stratified(
  skillId: string,
  entries: LabBenchV2Entry[],
  n: number,
): LabBenchV2Entry[] {
  const buckets = new Map<string, LabBenchV2Entry[]>()
  for (const entry of byHash(skillId, 'stratify', entries, (e) => e.key)) {
    const list = buckets.get(entry.bucket) ?? []
    list.push(entry)
    buckets.set(entry.bucket, list)
  }
  const order = byHash(skillId, 'buckets', [...buckets.keys()], (b) => b)
  const out: LabBenchV2Entry[] = []
  while (out.length < n && order.some((b) => buckets.get(b)!.length))
    for (const bucket of order) {
      const next = buckets.get(bucket)!.shift()
      if (next && out.length < n) out.push(next)
    }
  return out
}

export async function planBench(
  skill: SponsoredSkill,
  mode: BenchMode,
  options: { bump?: BenchBump; pool?: readonly LabBenchV2Entry[] } = {},
): Promise<BenchPlan> {
  const bump = options.bump ?? 'patch'
  const pool = options.pool ?? LAB_BENCH_V2
  const L = BENCH_PLAN_LIMITS

  const verdicts = new Map<string, BenchOracleVerdict>()
  const label = async (
    entry: LabBenchV2Entry,
    os: BenchOs,
    env: BenchEnvironment,
  ) => {
    const id = cellId(entry.key, os, env.key)
    let verdict = verdicts.get(id)
    if (!verdict) {
      verdict = await benchExpectation(
        skill,
        benchV2Snapshot(entry.key),
        os,
        env,
      )
      verdicts.set(id, verdict)
    }
    return verdict
  }

  const staticCells: BenchCell[] = []
  for (const entry of pool)
    for (const os of BENCH_OSES) {
      const env = benchDefaultEnvironment(
        os === 'linux' ? 'lab' : 'desktop',
        os,
      )
      staticCells.push(
        toCell(entry, os, 'static', 'default', await label(entry, os, env), 0),
      )
    }

  const liveTiers = mode === 'sweep' ? ['core', 'extended'] : ['core']
  const livePool = pool.filter((e) => liveTiers.includes(e.tier))
  const labPool = livePool.filter((e) => e.kind === 'github')
  const live: BenchCell[] = []
  const add = async (
    entry: LabBenchV2Entry,
    os: BenchOs,
    env: BenchEnvironment,
    attempts: number,
  ) => {
    const verdict = await label(entry, os, env)
    if (!verdict.applicable) return false
    const id = cellId(entry.key, os, env.key)
    if (live.some((c) => c.id === id)) return false
    live.push(toCell(entry, os, env.surface, env.key, verdict, attempts))
    return true
  }
  const labDefault = benchDefaultEnvironment('lab', 'linux')
  const runsOn = async (
    entry: LabBenchV2Entry,
    os: BenchOs,
    env: BenchEnvironment,
  ) => (await label(entry, os, env)).expected === 'should_run'

  // Lab, default environment.
  const labShouldRun: LabBenchV2Entry[] = []
  for (const entry of labPool)
    if (await runsOn(entry, 'linux', labDefault)) labShouldRun.push(entry)
  if (mode === 'smoke') {
    for (const entry of stratified(
      skill.id,
      labShouldRun,
      L.smokeLabRepositories,
    ))
      await add(entry, 'linux', labDefault, 1)
  } else {
    for (const entry of labShouldRun)
      await add(
        entry,
        'linux',
        labDefault,
        mode === 'full' ? L.fullAttempts : 1,
      )
  }

  // Lab environment cases, sampled where the skill runs.
  const labEnvs = BENCH_ENVIRONMENTS.filter(
    (env) =>
      env.surface === 'lab' &&
      env.key !== labDefault.key &&
      (mode === 'smoke' ? env.tier === 'smoke' : true),
  )
  const perEnv = mode === 'smoke' ? 1 : L.fullEnvironmentCells
  for (const env of labEnvs) {
    let picked = 0
    for (const entry of byHash(skill.id, env.key, labShouldRun, (e) => e.key)) {
      if (picked >= perEnv) break
      if (await add(entry, 'linux', env, 1)) picked++
    }
  }

  // Live refusals: the skill must stop itself on a repository it does not fit.
  if (mode !== 'smoke') {
    const refusing: LabBenchV2Entry[] = []
    for (const entry of labPool)
      if (
        (await label(entry, 'linux', labDefault)).expected === 'should_refuse'
      )
        refusing.push(entry)
    for (const entry of byHash(
      skill.id,
      'refuse',
      refusing,
      (e) => e.key,
    ).slice(0, L.fullRefusalCells))
      await add(entry, 'linux', labDefault, 1)
  }

  // Desktop shards.
  const surfaces = benchSurfaces(mode, bump)
  for (const os of BENCH_OSES) {
    if (!surfaces.includes(`desktop-${os}`)) continue
    const envs = BENCH_ENVIRONMENTS.filter(
      (env) => env.surface === 'desktop' && env.os === os,
    )
    const base = benchDefaultEnvironment('desktop', os)
    const shouldRun: LabBenchV2Entry[] = []
    for (const entry of livePool)
      if (await runsOn(entry, os, base)) shouldRun.push(entry)
    for (const entry of stratified(skill.id, shouldRun, L.desktop[os].default))
      await add(entry, os, base, 1)
    for (const env of envs.filter((e) => e.key !== base.key)) {
      let picked = 0
      for (const entry of byHash(skill.id, env.key, shouldRun, (e) => e.key)) {
        if (picked >= L.desktop[os].perEnvironment) break
        if (await add(entry, os, env, 1)) picked++
      }
    }
  }

  // Lab batches: one per environment, split to the Lab's caps. Each attempt
  // after the first is a re-run of that cell's failures, planned by the runner.
  const labBatches: BenchLabBatchPlan[] = []
  const byEnv = new Map<string, BenchCell[]>()
  for (const cell of live.filter((c) => c.surface === 'lab'))
    byEnv.set(cell.env, [...(byEnv.get(cell.env) ?? []), cell])
  const size = Math.min(LAB_BATCH_MAX_REPOSITORIES, LAB_BATCH_MAX_RUNS)
  for (const [envKey, cells] of byEnv) {
    const env = BENCH_ENVIRONMENTS.find((e) => e.key === envKey)!
    for (let i = 0; i < cells.length; i += size) {
      const chunk = cells.slice(i, i + size)
      labBatches.push({
        environment: env.lab!,
        repositories: chunk.map((c) => {
          const entry = pool.find((e) => e.key === c.entry)!
          if (entry.kind !== 'github')
            throw new Error(`${c.entry} is not on GitHub.`)
          return labBenchPinSpec(entry)
        }),
        cells: chunk.map((c) => c.id),
      })
    }
  }

  const desktopShards: BenchDesktopShardPlan[] = []
  for (const cell of live.filter((c) => c.surface === 'desktop')) {
    let shard = desktopShards.find(
      (s) =>
        s.os === cell.os &&
        s.env === cell.env &&
        s.entries.length < L.desktopShardEntries,
    )
    if (!shard) {
      shard = { os: cell.os, env: cell.env, entries: [], cells: [] }
      desktopShards.push(shard)
    }
    shard.entries.push(cell.entry)
    shard.cells.push(cell.id)
  }

  const labRuns = live
    .filter((c) => c.surface === 'lab')
    .reduce((n, c) => n + c.attempts, 0)
  const desktopRuns = Object.fromEntries(
    BENCH_OSES.map((os) => [
      os,
      live.filter((c) => c.surface === 'desktop' && c.os === os).length,
    ]),
  ) as Record<BenchOs, number>
  const githubMinutesLinuxEquivalent = BENCH_OSES.reduce(
    (n, os) =>
      n + desktopRuns[os] * L.desktopRunMinutes * L.githubMinuteMultiplier[os],
    0,
  )
  return {
    benchVersion: LAB_BENCH_V2_VERSION,
    mode,
    bump,
    skill: sponsoredSkillKey(skill),
    skillSha256: sponsoredSkillSha256(skill),
    static: staticCells,
    live,
    labBatches,
    desktopShards,
    estimate: {
      labRuns,
      desktopRuns,
      labWallMinutes:
        Math.ceil(labRuns / L.labConcurrentRuns) * L.labRunMinutes,
      // Shards run in parallel; the longest is one OS's shard of one env.
      desktopWallMinutes: Math.max(
        0,
        ...desktopShards.map((s) => s.entries.length * L.desktopRunMinutes),
      ),
      githubMinutesLinuxEquivalent,
    },
  }
}

/** The static labels' fingerprint, which a receipt records and CI recomputes. */
export function benchLabelsSha256(cells: readonly BenchCell[]): string {
  return sha256Hex(
    [...cells]
      .sort((a, b) => (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0))
      .map((c) => `${c.id}=${c.expected}`)
      .join('\n'),
  )
}

- File `common/src/ads/agentic-bench-receipt.ts` (274 lines):
```
/**
 * Bench receipts (bench-v2, B5): what one bench run of one skill version
 * found, committed next to the version as
 * `common/src/ads/sponsored-skills/<id>/<version>.bench.json` and indexed in
 * `sponsored-skills/receipts.ts`. CI checks the newest version of every skill
 * has a current one (`sponsored-skill-bench-receipt.test.ts`) and runs
 * nothing: the bench itself runs only when someone dispatches it.
 */
import {
  benchCellPass,
  type BenchCellResult,
  type BenchExpectation,
  type BenchRunOutcome,
} from './agentic-bench-oracle'
import { dropZ } from '../util/two-proportion-z'

import type { BenchOs, BenchSurface } from './agentic-bench-env'
import type { BenchBump, BenchMode, BenchPlan } from './agentic-bench-plan'

/** Smoke and full verdicts need at least this overall `should_run` pass rate. */
export const BENCH_MIN_PASS_RATE = 0.5
/** A drop of this many points in the `should_run` rate, at z ≥ BENCH_MIN_DROP_Z, regresses. */
export const BENCH_MAX_RATE_DROP = 0.1
export const BENCH_MIN_DROP_Z = 2
/** A cell regresses when it passed before and now passes 0 of at least this many attempts. */
export const BENCH_CELL_REGRESSION_ATTEMPTS = 3

export type BenchReceiptCell = {
  id: string
  entry: string
  os: BenchOs
  surface: BenchSurface
  env: string
  expected: BenchExpectation
  attempts: number
  passes: number
  results: BenchCellResult[]
  causeKeys: string[]
  /** One `gs://codebuff-ads-evals-text/bench/…` trace per attempt, when uploaded. */
  traces?: string[]
}

export type BenchVerdict = {
  result: 'pass' | 'regressed' | 'failed'
  /** The receipt it was compared with (`<id>@<version>`), or null for a first. */
  against: string | null
  reasons: string[]
}

export type BenchReceipt = {
  v: 1
  skill: string
  /** `sponsoredSkillSha256` of the version benched; must equal its lock line. */
  skillSha256: string
  benchVersion: string
  mode: BenchMode
  bump: BenchBump
  surfaces: string[]
  createdAt: string
  labBatchIds: string[]
  actionsRunUrl: string | null
  costUsd: number | null
  static: {
    cells: number
    byExpected: Record<BenchExpectation, number>
    labelsSha256: string
    fidelity: string[]
  }
  cells: BenchReceiptCell[]
  verdict: BenchVerdict
}

const rateOf = (cells: readonly BenchReceiptCell[]) => {
  const run = cells.filter((c) => c.expected === 'should_run')
  const hits = run.reduce((n, c) => n + Math.min(c.passes, 1), 0)
  return { hits, n: run.length, rate: run.length ? hits / run.length : null }
}

/** One cell's attempts folded: a pass stops the retries, a mismatch is never forgiven. */
export function benchReceiptCell(
  cell: Pick<
    BenchReceiptCell,
    'id' | 'entry' | 'os' | 'surface' | 'env' | 'expected'
  >,
  outcomes: readonly BenchRunOutcome[],
  traces?: string[],
): BenchReceiptCell {
  const results = outcomes.map((o) => benchCellPass(cell.expected, o))
  return {
    ...cell,
    attempts: results.filter((r) => r !== 'infra').length,
    passes: results.filter((r) => r === 'pass').length,
    results,
    causeKeys: outcomes.map((o) => o.causeKey ?? 'none'),
    ...(traces?.length ? { traces } : {}),
  }
}

/**
 * A receipt's own verdict, with no previous version: no label mismatch, at
 * least one pass in every language family the skill runs on, and an overall
 * `should_run` rate of at least BENCH_MIN_PASS_RATE.
 */
export function benchOwnVerdict(
  cells: readonly BenchReceiptCell[],
  families: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
): string[] {
  const reasons: string[] = []
  for (const cell of cells)
    if (cell.results.includes('mismatch'))
      reasons.push(
        `${cell.id}: expected ${cell.expected}, the run did the opposite`,
      )
  const run = cells.filter((c) => c.expected === 'should_run')
  const perFamily = new Map<string, number>()
  for (const cell of run) {
    const family = families.get(cell.entry) ?? 'other'
    perFamily.set(
      family,
      (perFamily.get(family) ?? 0) + Math.min(cell.passes, 1),
    )
  }
  for (const [family, passes] of perFamily)
    if (!passes) reasons.push(`No should_run cell passed for ${family}.`)
  const { rate } = rateOf(cells)
  if (rate !== null && rate < BENCH_MIN_PASS_RATE)
    reasons.push(
      `should_run pass rate ${(rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% is below ${BENCH_MIN_PASS_RATE * 100}%.`,
    )
  for (const cell of cells)
    if (cell.attempts === 0 && cell.results.length)
      reasons.push(`${cell.id}: every attempt was infra; not measured.`)
  return reasons
}

/**
 * THE regression rule between two receipts of the same skill (B5): a cell
 * regresses if it had at least one pass before and passes 0 of
 * BENCH_CELL_REGRESSION_ATTEMPTS now; the overall `should_run` rate
 * regresses when it drops BENCH_MAX_RATE_DROP or more at z ≥ BENCH_MIN_DROP_Z.
 * Only cells both receipts ran are compared.
 */
export function compareBenchReceipts(
  previous: Pick<BenchReceipt, 'skill' | 'cells'>,
  next: Pick<BenchReceipt, 'cells'>,
): { regressed: boolean; reasons: string[]; shared: number } {
  const reasons: string[] = []
  const before = new Map(previous.cells.map((c) => [c.id, c]))
  const shared = next.cells.filter((c) => before.has(c.id))
  for (const cell of shared) {
    const old = before.get(cell.id)!
    if (
      old.passes >= 1 &&
      cell.passes === 0 &&
      cell.attempts >= BENCH_CELL_REGRESSION_ATTEMPTS
    )
      reasons.push(
        `${cell.id}: passed ${old.passes}/${old.attempts} in ${previous.skill}, 0/${cell.attempts} now`,
      )
  }
  const a = rateOf(shared.map((c) => before.get(c.id)!))
  const b = rateOf(shared)
  if (a.rate !== null && b.rate !== null) {
    const drop = a.rate - b.rate
    const z = dropZ(a, b)
    if (drop >= BENCH_MAX_RATE_DROP && z >= BENCH_MIN_DROP_Z)
      reasons.push(
        `should_run rate fell ${(drop * 100).toFixed(0)} points (${(a.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}% → ${(b.rate * 100).toFixed(0)}%, z ${z.toFixed(1)})`,
      )
  }
  return { regressed: reasons.length > 0, reasons, shared: shared.length }
}

/** A receipt from a plan and its live outcomes, judged against the previous version's receipt. */
export function buildBenchReceipt(input: {
  plan: BenchPlan
  cells: BenchReceiptCell[]
  labelsSha256: string
  families: ReadonlyMap<string, string>
  previous: BenchReceipt | null
  labBatchIds: string[]
  actionsRunUrl: string | null
  costUsd: number | null
  createdAt: string
  surfaces?: string[]
}): BenchReceipt {
  const { plan } = input
  const byExpected = {
    should_run: 0,
    should_refuse: 0,
    should_fail_gracefully: 0,
  }
  for (const cell of plan.static) byExpected[cell.expected]++
  const fidelity = plan.static.flatMap((c) =>
    c.fidelity ? [`${c.id}: ${c.fidelity}`] : [],
  )
  const own = benchOwnVerdict(input.cells, input.families)
  if (fidelity.length)
    own.push(
      `${fidelity.length} static cell(s) where the fit oracle and the preflight disagree.`,
    )
  const compared = input.previous
    ? compareBenchReceipts(input.previous, { cells: input.cells })
    : null
  return {
    v: 1,
    skill: plan.skill,
    skillSha256: plan.skillSha256,
    benchVersion: plan.benchVersion,
    mode: plan.mode,
    bump: plan.bump,
    surfaces:
      input.surfaces ??
      [
        ...new Set(
          input.cells.map((c) =>
            c.surface === 'lab' ? 'lab' : `desktop-${c.os}`,
          ),
        ),
      ].sort(),
    createdAt: input.createdAt,
    labBatchIds: input.labBatchIds,
    actionsRunUrl: input.actionsRunUrl,
    costUsd: input.costUsd,
    static: {
      cells: plan.static.length,
      byExpected,
      labelsSha256: input.labelsSha256,
      fidelity,
    },
    cells: input.cells,
    verdict: {
      result: own.length
        ? 'failed'
        : compared?.regressed
          ? 'regressed'
          : 'pass',
      against: input.previous?.skill ?? null,
      reasons: [...own, ...(compared?.reasons ?? [])],
    },
  }
}

/** Markdown for a PR body, between `<!-- bench -->` markers the workflow rewrites. */
export function benchVerdictMarkdown(receipt: BenchReceipt): string {
  const lines = [
    '<!-- bench -->',
    `**Bench (${receipt.benchVersion}, ${receipt.mode}): ${receipt.verdict.result.toUpperCase()}**${receipt.verdict.against ? ` against ${receipt.verdict.against}` : ''}`,
    '',
    `Static oracle: ${receipt.static.cells} cells (${receipt.static.byExpected.should_run} should_run, ${receipt.static.byExpected.should_refuse} should_refuse, ${receipt.static.byExpected.should_fail_gracefully} should_fail_gracefully).`,
    '',
    '| Cell | Expected | Passes | Causes | Trace |',
    '| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |',
    ...receipt.cells.map(
      (c) =>
        `| ${c.id} | ${c.expected} | ${c.passes}/${c.attempts}${c.results.includes('mismatch') ? ' (mismatch)' : ''} | ${[...new Set(c.causeKeys)].join(', ')} | ${c.passes < c.attempts && c.traces?.length ? c.traces.map((t, i) => `[${i + 1}](${t.replace(/^gs:\/\//, 'https://storage.cloud.google.com/')})`).join(' ') : ''} |`,
    ),
    '',
    ...receipt.verdict.reasons.map((r) => `- ${r}`),
    ...(receipt.actionsRunUrl ? ['', `Run: ${receipt.actionsRunUrl}`] : []),
    '<!-- /bench -->',
  ]
  return lines.join('\n')
}

/** The body with its bench table replaced (or appended). */
export function withBenchVerdict(body: string, receipt: BenchReceipt): string {
  const table = benchVerdictMarkdown(receipt)
  const re = /<!-- bench -->[\s\S]*?<!-- \/bench -->/
  return re.test(body)
    ? body.replace(re, table)
    : `${body.trimEnd()}\n\n${table}\n`
}

- File `common/src/ads/agentic-bench-snapshot.ts` (42 lines):
```
/**
 * The static oracle's view of a bench entry (`agentic-bench-oracle.ts`):
 * every fit-file path at the pin (basenames in `SPONSORED_FIT_FILE_NAMES`),
 * with the text of the files the fit builder reads. Written by
 * `bun scripts/agentic-ad-lab-bench-verify.ts --bench=v2 --snapshots` into
 * `agentic-bench-v2-snapshots.json`; a test checks every pool entry has one.
 */
export type BenchSnapshot = {
  /** Every fit file at the pin is in `files` (none cut by the cap). */
  complete: boolean
  /**
   * Repo-relative path → text. `null`: the file is there but its text is not
   * kept (lockfiles), so a check reading it cannot be decided statically.
   */
  files: Record<string, string | null>
}

export type BenchSnapshots = {
  version: string
  entries: Record<string, BenchSnapshot>
}

export const BENCH_SNAPSHOT_MAX_PATHS = 400
export const BENCH_SNAPSHOT_FILE_BYTES = 64 * 1024

/** A snapshot's text as a Windows checkout (`core.autocrlf=true`) holds it. */
export function benchSnapshotForOs(
  snapshot: BenchSnapshot,
  os: 'darwin' | 'linux' | 'win32',
): BenchSnapshot {
  if (os !== 'win32') return snapshot
  return {
    complete: snapshot.complete,
    files: Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(snapshot.files).map(([path, text]) => [
        path,
        text === null ? null : text.replace(/\r?\n/g, '\r\n'),
      ]),
    ),
  }
}

- File `common/src/ads/agentic-bench-v2-snapshot-data.ts` (16 lines):
```
import SNAPSHOTS from './agentic-bench-v2-snapshots.json'

import type { BenchSnapshot, BenchSnapshots } from './agentic-bench-snapshot'

/**
 * The committed fit-file snapshots of every bench-v2 pool entry, written by
 * `bun scripts/agentic-ad-lab-bench-verify.ts --bench=v2 --snapshots`.
 */
export const BENCH_V2_SNAPSHOTS = SNAPSHOTS as BenchSnapshots

export function benchV2Snapshot(key: string): BenchSnapshot {
  const snapshot = BENCH_V2_SNAPSHOTS.entries[key.toLowerCase()]
  if (!snapshot) throw new Error(`No bench-v2 snapshot for ${key}.`)
  return snapshot
}

  endsAgentStep = true,
  exampleInputs = [],
  execute,

import { enableMapSet } from 'immer'

// Every store that drafts a Map or Set through immer enables the plugin itself.
// immer's Map/Set support is opt-in and lives on a PROCESS-GLOBAL registry, so
// enabling it from one place far away (this used to happen in init-app) made a
// store correct only once the app had booted: importing it directly threw
// "[Immer] minified error nr: 0", and its tests passed only when some other file
// happened to run first and enable the plugin. Doing it per store makes each one
// correct on its own import, and makes a store that forgets fail the same way
// everywhere instead of only outside the app. enableMapSet() is idempotent.
enableMapSet()

+import type { LabEnvironment } from './agentic-ad-lab-batch'
+
+/**
+ * The bench's environment axis (bench-v2, B3): what the machine around the
+ * repository looks like. Sampled, never crossed with every repository: the
+ * planner (`agentic-bench-plan.ts`) picks each case's live cells by
+ * `hash(skill.id, env)`, so successive versions of a skill hit the same cells.
+ *
+ * - `lab`: a Cloud Lab VM (Linux), set up by the Lab batch's environment case
+ *   (`LAB_ENVIRONMENTS`).
+ * - `desktop`: headless Freebuff Desktop on a GitHub runner
+ *   (`sponsored-skill-bench.yml`), with real containment (bwrap and a private
+ *   HOME on Linux), which the Lab cannot reproduce (the COD-825 shim failures).
+ *   `setup` runs on the runner before the leg. It may append `KEY=VALUE` lines
+ *   to `$BENCH_ENV_FILE` (the Desktop process's environment overrides; `PATH`
+ *   replaces it) and set `BENCH_PROJECT_PARENT=<dir>` there, the folder the
+ *   project is cloned under (paths with spaces, OneDrive, >260 characters).
+ *
+ * `hides` names the toolchains the case takes away. A cell whose run needs one
+ * is labelled `should_fail_gracefully` by the oracle until managed toolchains
+ * (spec E1) ship and `BENCH_HEALED_TOOLCHAINS` lists it.
+ */
+export type BenchSurface = 'lab' | 'desktop'
+export type BenchOs = 'linux' | 'win32' | 'darwin'
+export type BenchToolchain = 'node' | 'python' | 'pip'
+
+export type BenchEnvironment = {
+  key: string
+  surface: BenchSurface
+  os: BenchOs
+  /** smoke: in every smoke run; full: only in full runs and sweeps. */
+  tier: 'smoke' | 'full'
+  label: string
+  /** The Lab batch's environment case (surface `lab`). */
+  lab?: LabEnvironment
+  /** Runs on the GitHub runner before a Desktop leg (surface `desktop`). */
+  setup?: { shell: 'bash' | 'pwsh'; script: string }
+  hides: BenchToolchain[]
+  /** The case needs a root package.json (the collision writer edits it). */
+  needsRootPackageJson?: boolean
+}
+
+/** Toolchains a run installs for itself once spec E1 ships; empty until then. */
+export const BENCH_HEALED_TOOLCHAINS: readonly BenchToolchain[] = []
+
+const lab = (
+  key: LabEnvironment,
+  tier: BenchEnvironment['tier'],
+  label: string,
+  hides: BenchToolchain[] = [],
+  extra: Partial<BenchEnvironment> = {},
+): BenchEnvironment => ({
+  key: `lab:${key}`,
+  surface: 'lab',
+  os: 'linux',
+  tier,
+  label,
+  lab: key,
+  hides,
+  ...extra,
+})
+
+const desktop = (
+  os: BenchOs,
+  key: string,
+  label: string,
+  setup: BenchEnvironment['setup'] | undefined,
+  hides: BenchToolchain[] = [],
+): BenchEnvironment => ({
+  key: `desktop:${key}`,
+  surface: 'desktop',
+  os,
+  tier: 'full',
+  label,
+  ...(setup ? { setup } : {}),
+  hides,
+})
+
+/** PATH without any directory a Node install lives in (Linux and macOS runners). */
+const POSIX_PATH_WITHOUT_NODE = `p=$(printf %s "$PATH" | tr ':' '\\n' | grep -v -e hostedtoolcache -e '/usr/local/bin' -e nvm -e volta -e '/opt/homebrew' -e fnm -e asdf -e mise | paste -sd: -)
+echo "PATH=$p:/usr/bin:/bin" >> "$BENCH_ENV_FILE"`
+
+/** A manager activated only from the profile, the way a developer's shell does it. */
+const POSIX_MANAGER = (install: string, profile: string) => `set -eu
+${install}
+printf '%s\\n' ${profile
+  .split('\n')
+  .map((line) => `'${line}'`)
+  .join(' ')} >> "$HOME/.bashrc"
+printf '%s\\n' ${profile
+  .split('\n')
+  .map((line) => `'${line}'`)
+  .join(' ')} >> "$HOME/.zprofile"
+${POSIX_PATH_WITHOUT_NODE}`
+
+const WIN_PATH_WITHOUT = (pattern: string) =>
+  `$p = ($env:PATH -split ';' | Where-Object { $_ -and $_ -notmatch '${pattern}' }) -join ';'`
+

+export function benchEnvironment(key: string): BenchEnvironment {
+  const env = BENCH_ENVIRONMENTS.find((e) => e.key === key)
+  if (!env) throw new Error(`No bench environment ${key}.`)
+  return env
+}
+
+/** The case a surface and OS run when nothing else is asked for. */
+export function benchDefaultEnvironment(
+  surface: BenchSurface,
+  os: BenchOs,
+): BenchEnvironment {
+  const key =
+    surface === 'lab'
+      ? 'lab:default'
+      : os === 'win32'
+        ? 'desktop:win-default'
+        : os === 'darwin'
+          ? 'desktop:mac-homebrew'
+          : 'desktop:linux-default'
+  return benchEnvironment(key)
+}

+import type { LabBenchV2Entry } from './agentic-ad-lab-bench-v2'
+
+/** Each pool entry's language family, for "at least one pass per family". */
+export function benchLanguageFamilies(
+  pool: readonly LabBenchV2Entry[],
+): ReadonlyMap<string, string> {
+  return new Map(
+    pool.map((entry) => {
+      const { language } = entry.facets
+      const family =
+        language === 'python' || language === 'polyglot'
+          ? 'python'
+          : language === 'typescript' || language === 'javascript'
+            ? 'node'
+            : 'other'
+      return [entry.key, family]
+    }),
+  )

diff --git a/common/src/ads/agentic-bench-oracle.ts b/common/src/ads/agentic-bench-oracle.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..f1bca45a7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/ads/agentic-bench-oracle.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,278 @@
+/**
+ * The bench's static oracle (bench-v2, B2): what a skill SHOULD do on one
+ * bench cell (repository snapshot × OS × environment), decided with no run:
+ *
+ * - `should_refuse`: the skill's own preflight refuses the repository (or
+ *   the OS) before any edit. The real `runSponsoredSkillPreflight` decides
+ *   it over a static context of the snapshot, and Track A's fit oracle
+ *   (`sponsoredSkillFitRefusal` over `sponsoredRepoFacts`) must agree.
+ * - `should_fail_gracefully`: the skill fits, but the environment takes away
+ *   a toolchain the run needs (`BenchEnvironment.hides`), so the run should
+ *   stop cleanly, not crash or report a delivery.
+ * - `should_run`: neither; the run should deliver.
+ *
+ * Checks it cannot decide statically (commands, scripts, globs over source
+ * files, lockfile text it does not keep) are `unverified`, never a refusal:
+ * the live run decides them. Free and deterministic, so it covers the whole
+ * pool on three OSes on every bench run.
+ */
+import {
+  BENCH_HEALED_TOOLCHAINS,
+  type BenchEnvironment,
+  type BenchOs,
+  type BenchToolchain,
+} from './agentic-bench-env'
+import {
+  benchSnapshotForOs,
+  type BenchSnapshot,
+} from './agentic-bench-snapshot'
+import {
+  evaluateSponsoredSkillFit,
+  sponsoredFitCheckDecidable,
+  sponsoredRepoFacts,
+  sponsoredStaticCheckContext,
+  type SponsoredRepoFacts,
+  type SponsoredSkillFitVerdict,
+} from './sponsored-skill-fit'
+import {
+  sponsoredSkillFitEcosystems,
+  sponsoredSkillGlob,
+  sponsoredSkillPreflightChecks,
+  type SponsoredSkill,
+  type SponsoredSkillCheck,
+} from './sponsored-skill'
+import {
+  runSponsoredSkillPreflight,
+  SPONSORED_PREFLIGHT_OS_CHECK_ID,
+} from './sponsored-step-driver'
+
+export type BenchExpectation =
+  | 'should_run'
+  | 'should_refuse'
+  | 'should_fail_gracefully'
+
+export type BenchOracleVerdict = {
+  expected: BenchExpectation
+  /**
+   * False when the environment cannot be set up on this repository (the
+   * collision case needs a root package.json): the planner never runs it.
+   */
+  applicable: boolean
+  /** One line: the refusing check, the hidden toolchain, or `fits`. */
+  reason: string
+  /** The fit oracle's verdict on the same facts. */
+  fit: SponsoredSkillFitVerdict['fit']
+  /** Preflight checks the snapshot cannot decide; the live run decides them. */
+  unverified: string[]
+  /**
+   * The fit oracle and the real preflight disagree on a check both can
+   * decide: a bug in one of them. The label follows the real preflight.
+   */
+  fidelity?: string
+}
+
+/** The toolchains a run of `skill` on this repository needs. */
+export function benchRunToolchains(
+  skill: SponsoredSkill,
+  facts: SponsoredRepoFacts,
+): BenchToolchain[] {
+  const hasNode = facts.manifests.some((m) => m.ecosystem === 'npm')
+  const hasPython = facts.manifests.some((m) => m.ecosystem === 'pypi')
+  const routes = sponsoredSkillFitEcosystems(skill) ?? [
+    ...new Set(
+      skill.requirements.runtimes.map((r) =>
+        r.name === 'python' ? 'python' : 'node',
+      ),
+    ),
+  ]
+  // The route this repository takes: the first the skill offers that the
+  // repository has a manifest for, else the skill's first.
+  const route =
+    routes.find((r) => (r === 'node' ? hasNode : hasPython)) ?? routes[0]
+  const needs = new Set<BenchToolchain>()
+  if (route === 'node') needs.add('node')
+  if (route === 'python') {
+    needs.add('python')
+    if (skill.packages.some((p) => p.ecosystem === 'pypi')) needs.add('pip')
+  }
+  // An npm tool needs Node only where a check runs it; instructions that use
+  // it "when npx is available" do not make Node a requirement.
+  const npmTools = new Set(
+    skill.tools.filter((t) => t.source === 'npm').map((t) => t.name),
+  )
+  const checks = [
+    ...sponsoredSkillPreflightChecks(skill),
+    ...skill.steps.flatMap((step) => step.checks),
+  ]
+  if (
+    checks.some(
+      (check) =>
+        check.kind === 'command_succeeds' &&
+        (npmTools.has(check.argv[0] ?? '') ||
+          ['node', 'npm', 'npx'].includes(check.argv[0] ?? '')),
+    )
+  )
+    needs.add('node')
+  return [...needs]
+}
+
+const contentless = (snapshot: BenchSnapshot) =>
+  Object.entries(snapshot.files)
+    .filter(([, text]) => text === null)
+    .map(([path]) => path)
+
+/** Whether the snapshot can decide this check at all. */
+function staticallyDecidable(
+  check: SponsoredSkillCheck,
+  snapshot: BenchSnapshot,
+): boolean {
+  if (!sponsoredFitCheckDecidable(check)) return false
+  if (check.kind === 'file_contains') {
+    const re = sponsoredSkillGlob(check.path)
+    if (contentless(snapshot).some((path) => re.test(path))) return false
+  }
+  return true
+}
+
+export async function benchRepoFacts(
+  snapshot: BenchSnapshot,
+): Promise<SponsoredRepoFacts> {
+  return sponsoredRepoFacts({
+    paths: Object.keys(snapshot.files),
+    complete: snapshot.complete,
+    read: (path) => snapshot.files[path] ?? null,
+  })
+}
+
+/** THE label of one bench cell. */
+export async function benchExpectation(
+  skill: SponsoredSkill,
+  snapshot: BenchSnapshot,
+  os: BenchOs,
+  env: Pick<BenchEnvironment, 'hides' | 'needsRootPackageJson'>,
+): Promise<BenchOracleVerdict> {
+  const onOs = benchSnapshotForOs(snapshot, os)
+  const facts = await benchRepoFacts(onOs)
+  const fit = evaluateSponsoredSkillFit(skill, { os, repo: facts })
+  const applicable = !env.needsRootPackageJson || 'package.json' in onOs.files
+
+  const files = Object.fromEntries(
+    Object.entries(onOs.files).map(([path, text]) => [path, text ?? '']),
+  )
+  const preflight = await runSponsoredSkillPreflight({
+    skill,
+    ctx: sponsoredStaticCheckContext(files),
+    platform: os,
+  })
+  const declared = new Map(
+    sponsoredSkillPreflightChecks(skill).map((check) => [check.id, check]),
+  )
+  const unverified: string[] = []
+  const refused: string[] = []
+  for (const result of preflight.checks) {
+    if (result.status !== 'fail') continue
+    if (result.id === SPONSORED_PREFLIGHT_OS_CHECK_ID) {
+      refused.push(result.id)
+      continue
+    }
+    const check = declared.get(result.id)
+    if (check && staticallyDecidable(check, onOs)) refused.push(result.id)
+    else unverified.push(result.id)
+  }
+  // A check the static run did not fail can still be one it could not
+  // decide (a command it never ran because an earlier check failed).
+  for (const check of declared.values())
+    if (
+      !staticallyDecidable(check, onOs) &&
+      !unverified.includes(check.id) &&
+      !refused.includes(check.id) &&
+      check.kind !== 'no_privileged_secrets' &&
+      check.kind !== 'changes_within'
+    )
+      unverified.push(check.id)
+
+  let fidelity: string | undefined
+  if (fit.fit === 'misfit' && !refused.length)
+    fidelity = `the fit oracle refuses on ${fit.checkId} (${fit.reason}) but the real preflight passes every check it can decide`
+  if (fit.fit === 'fits' && refused.length)
+    fidelity = `the fit oracle says fits but the real preflight refuses on ${refused.join(', ')}`
+
+  const base = {
+    applicable,
+    fit: fit.fit,
+    unverified,
+    ...(fidelity ? { fidelity } : {}),
+  }
+  if (refused.length)
+    return {
+      ...base,
+      expected: 'should_refuse',
+      reason: `preflight refuses: ${refused.join(', ')}`,
+    }
+  const missing = benchRunToolchains(skill, facts).filter(
+    (tool) =>
+      env.hides.includes(tool) && !BENCH_HEALED_TOOLCHAINS.includes(tool),
+  )
+  if (missing.length)
+    return {
+      ...base,
+      expected: 'should_fail_gracefully',
+      reason: `the environment has no ${missing.join(' or ')}`,
+    }
+  return { ...base, expected: 'should_run', reason: 'fits' }
+}
+
+/** What one live run of a cell did, from a Lab grade or a Desktop leg's result. */
+export type BenchRunOutcome = {
+  tier: 'perfect' | 'handoff' | 'partial' | 'broken' | 'lost' | null
+  causeKey: string | null
+  /** The step driver's stop reason when known (`preflight_failed`, …). */
+  stoppedBecause?: string | null
+  /** The run never tested the skill: no grade, a failed setup, an infra cause. */
+  infra?: boolean
+}
+
+/** Grader causes that mean the run stopped at the skill's own refusal. */
+export const BENCH_REFUSAL_CAUSES: readonly string[] = [
+  'preflight_refused',
+  'not_a_fit',
+  'platform_unsupported',
+]
+
+/** Grader causes that mean a clean stop for a missing toolchain. */
+export const BENCH_GRACEFUL_CAUSES: readonly string[] = [
+  ...BENCH_REFUSAL_CAUSES,
+  'missing_node',
+  'missing_toolchain',
+  'environment_limited',
+]
+
+export type BenchCellResult = 'pass' | 'fail' | 'mismatch' | 'infra'
+
+/**
+ * THE pass predicate of one live run against its label. A run that did
+ * the opposite of its label (delivered where it should refuse, refused
+ * where it should run) is a `mismatch`, always red.
+ */
+export function benchCellPass(
+  expected: BenchExpectation,
+  outcome: BenchRunOutcome,
+): BenchCellResult {
+  if (outcome.infra || !outcome.tier) return 'infra'
+  const refused =
+    outcome.stoppedBecause === 'preflight_failed' ||
+    BENCH_REFUSAL_CAUSES.includes(outcome.causeKey ?? '')
+  switch (expected) {
+    case 'should_run':
+      if (outcome.tier === 'perfect') return 'pass'
+      return refused ? 'mismatch' : 'fail'
+    case 'should_refuse':
+      if (refused) return 'pass'
+      return outcome.tier === 'perfect' ? 'mismatch' : 'fail'
+    case 'should_fail_gracefully':
+      if (outcome.tier === 'perfect') return 'mismatch'
+      return refused || BENCH_GRACEFUL_CAUSES.includes(outcome.causeKey ?? '')
+        ? 'pass'
+        : 'fail'
+  }
+}
